Let x = n * n.
Let a = shift_sint32(board_0, 0).
Let a_1 = memcpy(Mint_0, a, Mint_undef_0, a, x).
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(i) /\ is_sint32(n).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(board_0.base) <= 0) /\ linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_valid_board(Malloc_0, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: 0 <= x.
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: (0 <= i) /\ (i <= x).
  (* Invariant 'i_2' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < i) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, i_1)] = 0))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: x <= i.
}
Prove: P_zero_board(a_1, n, board_0).

      ((i < n) -> ((i + (n * i_1)) <= x))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)
  Have: (0 <= j) /\ (j <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_22' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_3, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_23' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_3, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_3, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_25' *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(a_3, n, board_0, x_3).
  (* Invariant 'i_26' *)
  Have: forall i : Z. ((0 <= i) -> ((i < j) ->
      (a_3[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i))] = 0))).
  (* Invariant 'i_27' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z.
      let a_4 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_1, i)) in ((0 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) -> ((i < n) ->
      (a_3[a_4] = Mint_0[a_4]))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_41' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) != x_2))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_47' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_1, i) < x_2))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_57' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> ((i + (n * i_1)) < x_1))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_64' *)
  Have: forall i_1,i : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i) ->
      ((i < n) -> ((i + (n * i_1)) <= x_1))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n <= j.
  (* Assertion *)

  Have: (0 <= row_0) /\ (row_0 < n).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_binary_board(Mint_0, n, board_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_0, n, board_0, x_4).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_0, n, board_0, x_4).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_0, n, board_0, x_4).
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)
  Have: 0 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_0, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_41' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_3, i_2) != x_6))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_47' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> (L_idx(n, i_3, i_2) < x_6))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_57' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> ((i_2 + (n * i_3)) < x_2))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_62' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 < row_0) ->
      ((n + (n * i_2)) <= x_2))).
  (* Invariant 'i_63' *)
  Have: 0 < n.
  (* Invariant 'i_64' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < row_0) -> ((0 <= i_2) ->
      ((i_2 < n) -> ((i_2 + (n * i_3)) <= x_2))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)
  Have: (0 <= j) /\ (j <= n).
  (* Invariant 'i_22' *)
  Have: P_binary_board(a_1, n, board_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_23' *)
  Have: P_rows_solved(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(a_1, n, board_0, row_0).

  (* Invariant 'i_26' *)

      (a_1[shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, row_0, i_2))] = 0))).
  (* Invariant 'i_27' *)

      let a_3 = shift_sint32(board_0, L_idx(n, i_3, i_2)) in ((0 <= i_3) ->

      (a_1[a_3] = Mint_0[a_3]))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_41' *)

  (* Invariant 'i_47' *)

  Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_1, n, board_0, row_0).
  If row_0 = n
  Then { Have: Mint_1 = Mint_0. }
  Else {
    Let x = 1 + row_0.
    Let x_1 = n * x.
    Let a = shift_sint32(board_0, x_1).
    Let x_2 = n * n.
    Let x_3 = n * row_0.
    Let a_1 = shift_sint32(board_0, x_3).
    Have: Mint_1 = Mint_2.
    Have: Malloc_0 = Malloc_1.
    (* Invariant 'i_28' *)
    Have: P_valid_board(Malloc_1, n, board_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_29' *)
    Have: row_0 < n.
    (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
    Have: 0 <= n.
    (* Invariant 'i_31' *)
    Have: P_binary_board(Mint_2, n, board_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_32' *)
    Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_2, n, board_0, row_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_33' *)
    Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_2, n, board_0, row_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_34' *)
    Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_2, n, board_0, row_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_30' *)
    Have: (0 <= col_0) /\ (col_0 <= n).
    (* Invariant 'i_31' *)
    Have: P_binary_board(Mint_3, n, board_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_32' *)
    Have: P_rows_solved(Mint_3, n, board_0, row_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_33' *)
    Have: P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_3, n, board_0, row_0).
    (* Invariant 'i_34' *)
    Have: P_rows_empty_from(Mint_3, n, board_0, row_0).
    Have: col_0 < n.
    Have: (ta_tmp_0_0=false) /\
        (memcpy(Mint_2, a_1, Mint_undef_0, a_1, x_2 - x_3) = Mint_3).
    Have: (ta_tmp_0=false).
    (* Assertion *)
    Have: (n + x_3) <= x_2.
    (* Call 'place_queen' *)
    Have: P_binary_board(Mint_4, n, board_0) /\
        P_safe_position(Mint_3, n, board_0, row_0, col_0) /\
        P_rows_solved(Mint_4, n, board_0, x) /\
        P_rows_empty_from(Mint_4, n, board_0, x) /\
        P_no_attack_prefix(Mint_4, n, board_0, x).
    (* Call Effects *)
    Have: memcpy(Mint_3, a_1, Mint_undef_1, a_1, n) = Mint_4.
    (* Assertion *)
    Have: x_1 <= x_2.
    (* Call 'solve_from_row' *)
    Have: ((-1) <= row_0) /\ P_n_queen_solution(Mint_0, n, board_0).
    (* Call Effects *)
    Have: memcpy(Mint_4, a, Mint_undef_2, a, x_2 - x_1) = Mint_0.
  }
}
Prove: P_n_queen_solution(Mint_0, n, board_0).
